### Footnotes

[^1]: While the vanilla JP form of 立 is acceptable and arguably preferred, many JP glyphs with similar components (e.g. 䒑, 豆, etc.) do not adopt such forms, possibly due to time constraint when the vanilla font was adapted from Kozuka Gothic, which fundamentally has the third bending stroke of 立 not connecting to the bottom horizontal stroke. Thus the change was made to 立 for more consistency and bringing back the original JP design.
